All assets within this project are licensed under Creative Commons
Attribution Share-Alike 4.0 (CC-BY-SA 4.0) unless otherwise noted.

<https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/>

If you wish to use parts of the game in your own work, please credit the
origial author(s) according to CC BY-SA 4.0 (or compatible). To help with
attribution you may use the following disclaimer, adapted to whose assets
you may be using.

	Based on <ASSET TYPE> by <CONTRIBUTORS> for "Weissblatt - The Asura Incident"
	Weissblatt - The Asura Incident © 2023-2025 Liquid Underground. Licensed under Creative Commons Attribution Share-Alike 4.0. No warranties.
	<https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/>

Names for our contributors follow one of the following patterns. If a
contributor usually puts their family name first for cultural reasons (e.g.
japanese names), the family name is CAPITALIZED:

1. <FIRST NAME> "<NICKNAME>" <(CAP.) FAMILY NAME>
2. <FIRST NAME> <(CAP.) FAMILY NAME>
3. "<NICKNAME>"
4. <CAP. FAMILY NAME> "<NICKNAME>" <FIRST NAME>
5. <CAP. FAMILY NAME> <FIRST NAME>

If you use or adapt assets of multiple types, you may simply refer to them
as "assets". If you use assets from multiple contributors, you may list
them in one collective disclaimer for the project. Example:

	Based on assets by Alice, Bob and Charlie for "Weissblatt - The Asura Incident"
	Weissblatt - The Asura Incident © 2023-2025 Liquid Underground. Licensed under Creative Commons Attribution Share-Alike 4.0. No warranties.
	<https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/>

2. Credits by asset
-------------------

Below you can see a tab-separated table of every asset and it's
contributor(s).  A "*" at the end signifies a catchall for every file,
similar to UNIX globbing.

The directory `/TEXTURES` does not exist and denotes virtual textures
created through a ´TEXTUREDEF` file. If already covered, the explicit
filename will be given in place of the directory.
